| AWR-148 | RBMEM scan vectorization (kill the multi-second freezes from deck-2 playhead scans) | `docs/plans/active/rbmem_scan_vectorization_spec.md` | **Implemented/software-tested 2026-07-08** via the operator-sanctioned delegate lane (Codex down until Jul 11); spec authored 2026-07-08 (executive session), Tasks 1-3 one commit each (`18cd930`, `2f43a4b`, `4a827f7`). As built: the shared numeric pre-filters `_i32_moving_candidates` / `_i32_static_candidates` have a numpy fast path (int64-cast subtract so int32 wraparound matches the big-int loop; mask chain + `np.nonzero`; measured 28-40× — 1.9 ms / 10.6 ms) and a pure fallback that yields the GIL every `_SLICE_INTS=16384` ints; numpy is a lazy OPTIONAL import (`_numpy()`, never a hard dependency) and any numpy error falls back to the pure loop for that call. The three scans call the helpers for the int32 pre-filter and keep every per-candidate pointer/`_is_objc`/secondary check, dedupe/sort/cap, retry cadence, and log line unchanged; the heap scan also yields the GIL between chunks. Candidate VALUES and ORDER are byte-identical to the old loop — proven by an old-loop oracle equivalence test incl. the int32-overflow edge and a wrap-trap case (numpy == fallback == oracle). Part D: `tests/test_rb_memory_scans.py` (10 tests) green; full `discover` at the known baseline (the 6 pre-existing environmental reds are all in soundswitch/laser-color/LED-Patch-D/export-pack, none in a reader). Mechanism, verified at `1b51c66`: the gate is a stateless 29 ms ON window (`(beat % 0.25) < 0.0625`, `govee_frame_renderer.py:511`, constants designed for 40 fps) sampled by a runner that holds the last frame through any stall with no catch-up (`govee_realtime_runner.py:251-265`) — under the AWR-151 throttle (28-33 fps + jitter) the offline sampling model reproduces his exact words: up to ~33% of strobe cycles show NO flash (runs of 5 = 0.6 s dead), and a stall on an ON frame holds full white ~165 ms (5.6× the designed flash). **Key finding: the ProcessType fix alone is NOT sufficient — at a jittery 60 fps the model still misses ~31% of cycles and holds white ~147 ms.** Robust fix direction (named, not specced): runner injects real frame timing into effect params (the `slot_colors` runtime-injection pattern) and the gate widens its ON window to ≥ ~1.5 frames so every cycle lands a flash; a stall cap keys off frame_index. **BINDING DESIGN INPUT (operator ruling 2026-07-08, mid-pad-session): the fixed gate is TIME-BASED (real Hz + duty), NOT beat/BPM-subdivided — identical feel at any BPM; lab-verified same session (Hz-clock lab strobes measure equal flashes/sec at 128 and 90 BPM).** The operator-requested 7-colorway full-strip strobe family (blue, cyan, green, red, red+white, blue+cyan, cyan+white; taste drafts live in Template Lab under AWR-152) rides this fix at promotion so the family is born robust.
